### C-7G — Meaning Engine Interior + Acceptance Check + Creation-aware mode (§7G)
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The SMART per-pass realization of the Universal Filter and Meaning Engine web chain. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — One target root, optional positional context, optional semantic context and a declared reading mode, purpose or angle. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Does: DESIGNED — Runs the one combined reading pass through a mouth proposal and an explicit independent acceptance check before a reading is written. Applies creation-aware mode to live-chat material Ness produces. A web that cannot be filled produces an informed gap, not a stop-and-ask demand. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — One accepted reading per pass, or the distinct governed failure or honest revisable insufficient-context outcome. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Replace the Universal Filter, run the webs as separate sequential engines, overwrite an earlier reading, create hidden secondary interpretations or let the mouth approve itself alone. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Fails closed by: DESIGNED — Rejects failed proposals with their specific recorded reasons; never turns rejected content silently into a reading or uses confident invention to fill a gap. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]

### C-7G.1 — One reading per declared pass
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §7G]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The scope and preservation contract of one reading pass. [V10 §7G]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — One target root and the pass's declared mode, purpose or angle, with its exact supplied context. [V10 §7G]
- Does: DESIGNED — Produces one reading record. Multiple angles require multiple explicit passes; each reading retains its own context inputs, engine version, configuration and timestamp. Multiple readings may point to the same root while remaining separate. Later synthesis is a separate layer. [V10 §7G]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — An independently traceable reading beside earlier readings. [V10 §7G]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Overwrite a prior reading, silently merge conflicting readings, hide secondary interpretations outside the reading record or let later synthesis rewrite existing readings. [V10 §7G]
- Fails closed by: NOT DECIDED

TOGETHER
- Fed by: ACCEPTED — C-7G.1.1 — Target-only reading without surroundings: Uses the target-only branch when surroundings are unavailable. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]
- Gated by: NOT DECIDED
- Changes: NOT DECIDED

### C-7G.1.1 — Target-only reading without surroundings
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The same reading process when no surrounding context is available. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — The target Origin alone and the reason surroundings are unavailable. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Records the absence and its reason, reads only what the target itself supports, and marks the result context-limited and revisable. Later context may produce a new reading beside it. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — An honest target-only interpretation with its context limitation recorded. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Invent missing meaning or replace the earlier reading when more context arrives. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Leaves unsupported meaning unasserted and retains an honestly limited revisable reading. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3]

TOGETHER
- Fed by: DESIGNED — C-7G.1 — One reading per declared pass: Preserves the one-pass and non-overwrite rule without surroundings. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_POLICY_DECISIONS_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4 / A3] [V10 §7G]

### C-7G.2 — Proposal-to-reading flow
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The ordered interior flow of the reading engine. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — Target root, optional positional context, optional semantic context and declared mode. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Does: DESIGNED — Sends the supplied inputs to the mouth as a proposal-producing step, then runs the Reading Proposal Acceptance Check, then permits a reading record only through the accepted path. The channels retain their distinct roles. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — A checked reading handoff or a separate failed-proposal outcome. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Write the mouth's unchecked proposal as the accepted reading or confuse supplied semantic context with preceding conversational context. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Fails closed by: DESIGNED — A failed proposal is rejected before the reading handoff. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]

### C-7G.3 — Confidence never authorizes acceptance
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §7G]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The rule that model confidence is metadata for every present and future model. [V10 §7G]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — A proposal, its claimed confidence, the root, supplied context, declared mode and explicit evidence. [V10 §7G]
- Does: DESIGNED — Judges acceptance from grounding and evidence rather than model self-description. Records why the proposal passed or failed. Treats unsupported certainty as grounds for rejection or downgrade; prefers honest uncertainty to confident fabrication. [V10 §7G]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — A reasoned acceptance outcome independent of the mouth's self-confidence. [V10 §7G]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Accept solely because a model is confident, treat a larger model as an exception, or record only a verdict without its reasoning. [V10 §7G]
- Fails closed by: DESIGNED — Rejects or downgrades unsupported certainty; confidence cannot supply missing evidence. [V10 §7G]

TOGETHER
- Fed by: DESIGNED — C-7G.4 — Seven minimum acceptance checks: Uses explicit acceptance findings as the authority over confidence. [V10 §7G]
- Gated by: NOT DECIDED
- Changes: NOT DECIDED

### C-7G.4 — Seven minimum acceptance checks

### C-7G.5 — Rejection and genuine insufficiency remain separate
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The failure boundary following a failed acceptance check. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — A failed proposal and the actual reason it could not pass. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Does: DESIGNED — Rejects the proposal and records the specific reason separately. Distinguishes fabrication, unsupported certainty, positional/semantic confusion, reading-mode violation, malformed output and incomplete output. Uses `insufficient_context` only when the available root/context genuinely cannot ground interpretation; that outcome is an honest written reading marked revisable. A rejected proposal may be retained in an audit log. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — A distinct rejection record or the separately governed honest insufficient-context reading. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Turn a rejected proposal into the accepted reading, disguise a defect as evidence insufficiency, invent a reading, or silently retry before a governing bounded rule is available. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]
- Fails closed by: DESIGNED — A substantive rejection is terminal for its attempt; a fresh proposal requires the accepted bounded retry boundary and its separate integration. [V10 §7G] [MAP C-7G]

### C-7G.8.5 — Grounding dimension separations
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §7]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The boundaries separating grounding from other judgments. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — Grounding, truth claims, telling firmness, confidence metadata, relevance, source reliability and validation results. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Keeps each axis in its own role. Firmness describes how strongly the perspective owner holds a stance; a firm stance can be ungrounded. Relevance and source reliability do not move grounding. Validation means only that no rule-detectable error was found. Repetition, restatement and re-logging add no certainty. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — A grounding status that neither absorbs nor rewrites the other dimensions. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Treat grounding as truth, firmness as support, confidence as authority, relevance as grounding, or derived material as independent evidence for its own originating interpretation. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Circular or unsupported claims gain no grounding through these other dimensions. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §7]

- Gated by: NOT DECIDED
- Changes: NOT DECIDED

### C-7G.8.6 — Grounding versus hold decision
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §6]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The fork between assessing present grounding and waiting for obtainable evidence. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §6]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — Missing context and the A29 test: a specific missing condition that is realistically, plausibly satisfiable later. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §6]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Uses the existing hold machinery only when both conditions hold and waiting can realistically help. Otherwise produces the honest insufficiency or unsupported-claim outcome through the owning machinery. A grounding status alone cannot open, block or release a hold. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §6]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — A governed hold or an honest present outcome, without a permanent review backlog. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §6]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Park a rejection in a hold, create endless waiting where evidence is not obtainable, or require Ness to manually review every hold. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §6]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — A missing useful condition or unrealistic obtainability does not authorize a hold; its release still requires the existing evidence-verified interface. [04/NH_A31_GROUNDED_ENOUGH_THRESHOLD_POLICY_v1_0_CANDIDATE.md §6]

Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The accepted design boundary for a fresh attempt after a bad, unsafe or unsupported B24 proposal. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — The exact durable rejection record, adopted retry rule, per-case authorization, episode budget and actual operation state. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Allows exactly one careful retry after recording the rejected draft and distinct reason, with both attempt and elapsed-time gates. A passing retry resumes ordinary processing; another rejection is terminal for that episode unless a later recorded real change authorizes a new bounded episode. The fallback opens only at its separately defined eligible B24 point and never proves insufficiency by timing alone. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — An admitted careful attempt, a refused/exhausted/early-stopped episode, or an eligible separately governed fallback assessment. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Turn a rejected draft into the answer, loop indefinitely, retry around a hold or authorization failure, conceal failure, or make technical recovery semantic retry permission. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — No required rejection or authorization record means no admission; a closed bound stops retry, and protected outcomes keep their own classifications. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

### C-7G.9.1 — Rejection recorded before careful retry
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The durable per-case authorization for the one careful retry. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — The rejected proposal/draft and its exact distinct rejection reason. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Commits the rejection through B24 first. Binds `retry_policy_ref` to the accepted rule and `retry_authorization_ref` to that exact rejection record; the careful attempt must take the record as context so the defect is visible to the producing pass. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — An evidence-bound admission basis for this case. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Admit before the rejection record exists, substitute another case's rejection or omit the defect from the careful attempt's context. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Refuses admission without the durable case record and adopted policy reference. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

### C-7G.9.3 — Careful-retry elapsed bound
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The real deadline bounding a substantive retry episode. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — The first durable rejection starting the episode and its fixed live-chat or background/nightly class. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Starts the elapsed clock at that rejection: 7 minutes for live chat, 15 minutes for background/nightly. Admits only while now is before the deadline and the attempt allowance is also open; whichever bound closes first stops further retry. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — A time-bounded episode independent of waiting-gap arithmetic. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Restart the clock on retry, treat a waiting gap as the deadline or use immediate retry to bypass elapsed exhaustion. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — If the deadline has passed before admission, records truthful time exhaustion and starts no retry. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]

### C-7G.9.4 — Immediate careful retry timing
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — Readiness-based timing after the durable rejection. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — The first admission evaluation after the required rejection record exists. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Applies no scheduled waiting gap to the careful retry; evaluates the count and deadline gates at that first durable-readiness opportunity. If the deadline passes while an admitted attempt runs, lets the source operation finish under its own timeout and terminal rules and admits no further retry afterward. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — An immediately eligible, still bounded attempt or an honest refusal. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Promise a wall-clock instant, start before durable readiness, force-cancel an in-flight source operation merely because the retry deadline passes or override its timeout rules. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — A delay, crash or recovery that misses the deadline prevents the retry from starting. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §7]

Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The outcome handling of the authorized careful retry. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

- Gives out: ACCEPTED — A valid continuation or a terminal rejected episode. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Treat a second rejection as success or use B-HOLD to park it. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Stops the rejected episode without another same-episode retry. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

### C-7G.9.6 — Eligible B24 fallback point
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The proposed narrowed timing boundary for insufficiency assessment or deterministic fallback. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — An eligible B24 proposal whose durable rejection exists, whose one authorized careful retry was consumed and rejected again, and whose B24 fallback conditions are reached. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Allows the separate B24 assessment/fallback machinery to open only after that sequence truthfully ended. Treats reaching this point as a timing fact; only a committed external `genuine_insufficiency_confirmed` may produce `insufficient_context`. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — Permission to invoke the separate governed assessment, never a pre-decided insufficiency result. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Open fallback merely because a class receives no B9 retry, infer missing evidence from rejection count or relabel a rejected proposal as an honest fallback. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — A missing eligible sequence or unmet B24 condition leaves fallback closed. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §13]

### C-7G.9.8 — Retry early stop and real-change continuation
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — Honest stopping when another retry is useless or unsafe. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — The recorded finding that retry is `clearly_useless` or `unsafe`, with its evidence. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Records proposed `retry_early_stopped`, ends admission for that episode and closes its requesting operation under the existing terminal contract. Exhaustion or early stop preserves the work and unfinished state, states why through the applicable surface rules, and permits later continuation only when a real recorded change exists. Examples include new memory, a new Ness instruction, better context, fixed evidence, repaired tool/file/search work or a solved blocker; the list is non-exhaustive. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — An honestly stopped episode and preserved continuation state. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Disguise early stop as exhaustion or success, treat repetition or time alone as a real change, or restart a stopped episode without the governed new-episode boundary. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — No real recorded change means no continuation after the stopped episode. [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §4] [04/NH_B9_RETRY_VALUES_WIRING_INTO_B9_B10_BHOLD_B24_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §8]

- Does: ACCEPTED — Runs Stage 1 → pre-assessment configuration check → Stage 2 → post-assessment independence check → Stage-3 external adjudication → verification of committed adjudication before payload creation. DUMB stages compare recorded identifiers, states and checks; governed SMART assessment proposes semantic findings. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — A separately committed validation result with every substantive, authorization, boundary and operational class preserved. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Require a record before its producing stage can exist, let a provider approve itself, treat a merely different model as sufficient validation or choose the still-open reviewer/disagreement policy. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Missing authorized configuration or unmet independent validation conditions leave the proposal unvalidated; no payload is authorized. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3]

Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3.1]

- What it is: ACCEPTED — Proposal-only mechanical checks over the brief and already committed references and authorization/operation records. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3.1]

- Does: ACCEPTED — Runs the eighteen named checks. Each yields PASS or its distinct reason. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3.1]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — Deterministic findings routed to the correct outcome space. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3.1]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Decide meaning, ask future provider records to validate an earlier stage, or populate rejection fields for a non-proposal failure. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §3.1]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The accepted transaction, stable-identity and recovery boundaries consumed by B24 validation and assessments. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.1] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.2] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.3] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.4]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — Exact parent/child identities, committed revisions, immutable brief/result records and durable destination evidence. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.1] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.2] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.3] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.4]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Uses the relevant complete-or-not-at-all commits, compound idempotency keys and lookup-first recovery. Each child preserves one terminal; completed replays return the committed result, in-progress work follows its recoverable state, and a failed, abandoned or cancelled child is never silently mutated into success. A failed child does not automatically terminate its parent. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.1] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.2] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.3] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.4]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — Recoverable validation/assessment artifacts without duplicate effects or rewritten history. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.1] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.2] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.3] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.4]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Claim an artifact past an uncommitted boundary exists, repeat an uncertain effect blindly, treat crash recovery as semantic retry permission or adopt an implementation technology through these contracts. [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.1] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.2] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.3] [04/NH_B24_VALIDATOR_FIRST_MODEL_BOUNDARY_AND_BENCHMARK_v7_CANDIDATE.md §6.4]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Unreadable or undeterminable effects fail closed to their honest unresolved outcome; late work from superseded parents or disqualified revisions is rejected. ## Scope and path placement

C-7G owns the declared-pass Meaning Engine interior, independent proposal acceptance, story grounding and creation-aware mode. Its current descendants carry the accepted A31 qualitative grounding policy, B9 acceptance-specific retry/fallback seam and the validation-facing B24 contract. These are design records; no model, validator implementation or runtime configuration is adopted by this chapter.

C-7G has separate USED BY rows for P-MAIN step 11, CY-A and CY-B. Every current descendant inherits those placements through the declared sub-part tree. Full CY-A/CY-B path assembly remains CH11. The post-root worker owns the operational calling sequence in CH05-b; context retrieval is CH05-c; complete generic retry architecture is CH05-d; the Unified Creation Store is CH05-e.

Earlier story evidence and telling-acceptance atoms retain C-ENGINE-C.2, C-ENGINE-C.3 and C-READ.10.3.1; hold machinery retains C-7B.7. Shared outcome atoms retain one ID across the validation, insufficiency and compatibility records. The current three record families do not acquire each other's terminal values merely because they reuse the four genuinely shared operational terminals.

## Explicit remaining scope

- **CH05-b, C-7GA:** complete queue/worker/mode assignment, reading-stage lifecycle, operation sentinels and crash recovery. **CH05-c, C-7F:** complete retrieval and context envelopes. **CH05-d, C-7H:** full B9 architecture, all technical admissions, proposed `b9_retry_budget_config` fields, episode/admission/event schemas, scheduler/hold interactions, exhaustion and new-episode lifecycle. Current numerical deadline consumption does not claim those complete records are already written here.
- **CH05-e, C-CREATE:** complete B14 store and creation record schema, type atoms, provisional/confirmed states and transitions. Existing live creation routing is CH04-d. A13's future interface behavior remains **CH10-e, C-19**.
- **CH10-b, C-16:** B24 §§2.1–2.7 full producer-brief, source-reference, facts/possibilities, unknowns, clarification and reply-direction schemas; §§2.11/2.11A capture/routing records; §4.2–4A complete sanitized-payload, evidence-basis and multilingual presentation contracts; §§5/5A/5B post-messenger checks, pending utterance and heavy-unavailable paths. The nine `MESSENGER_…` reasons and `WITHHELD_BY_GATE`, `UNRESOLVED_OUTCOME`, `DELIVERY_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`, `classification_unresolved` and delivery-specific terminals belong there with their full behavior.
- **CH10-b, C-16:** B24 §6 full parent/child architecture and all child types, T1/T2 and T8–T11, remaining idempotency rows, safe delivery, new-input decision and supplement operation, final output gates; §§7.1–7.6 complete parent/child terminal schemas, the other component events, proposed `operation_record_link`, `record_state_event` and `cooling_rule_record` schemas and every cooling-rule proposal/approval/versioning condition. Current validation-family child events consume that shared discipline without inventing a second parent log. **CH11** owns complete CY-B assembly. The final privacy-first/SACL output conflict already carried in CH04-c is not silently resolved here; its full source comparison follows those output owners.
- **CH10-b, C-16:** B24 §§7B/7C benchmark/test suites and model-candidate selection, complete §8 model/cycle wiring and open delivery/runtime choices. These benchmark sections were not read for this piece and receive no whole-file credit. B24 §§A/0/10–end likewise receive no current read credit; their behavior-bearing content remains for that full source review and project workflow is excluded under contract §1.3.
- **CH08-a, C-7Q** and **CH08-c, C-LMAC:** full privacy and internal-use authorization. **CH06-a, C-7J** owns conflict policy. Current acceptance preserves their boundaries rather than supplying new permissions. **CH03-c/d** already own the complete A2 telling semantic/assembly contracts; only acceptance is reused here. Other Bundle 6 policies remain with their assigned owners.
- **CH12:** cumulative undecided/source-conflict/coverage registers and audit corrections. The candidate's NOTE-policy question remains with **C-7A.8.4 and C-7B.11 / their already assigned NOTE scope**, not a new B24 permission to preserve rejected content as an N.H view.

## Review of plain gates and empty boxes

Every populated TOGETHER line names its actual rule, record field, criterion, state or connected owner. Criterion fields contain the actual precondition; parent/child links preserve distinct data flow and gating rather than relabeling an output as a gate. Explicit flow, state-transition, assessment-commit, retry, recovery and log-event steps link their owning rules. Atomic identity/version/time fields retain NOT DECIDED where the source specifies no independent failure mechanism or prohibition; parent validation and transaction rules remain present. No schema field silently acquires a new runtime permission.

All current cards, all USED BY cells and all empty restriction/failure/gate boxes were read against the mapped sections. The full B24 schema names retain their proposed status, the seven-check mapping is explicit, source category placeholders remain visible, and recovery preserves known absence versus unknown effects. Earlier fingerprints are listed and rechecked without editing earlier files.
